\textbf{(b)} Determine the gradient of function \(f(x,y)\) at the given point \(A\). \\[1mm]
\textbf{(c)} Write the equation of the tangent plane to the graph of the function \(g(x,y)\) at the point of tangency \(D\). \par \columnbreak
\textbf{(d)} Using linear approximation, estimate the value of the function \(g(x,y)\) at the point shifted by the vector \((\Delta x, \Delta y)\) from point \(D_0\). \\[1mm]
\textbf{(e)} Calculate \(h(x,y)\) at its local extremum. \\[1mm]
\textbf{(f)} Find the point of the constrained local extremum of the function \(h(x,y)\) on the given line \(y(x)\). \\[1mm]
\end{multicols}
